<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Stock Options</span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company grants stock options as part of employee
compensation and recognizes these options&#8217; expense over the vesting period. If an employee does not meet certain conditions such
as sales targets or leaves the Company before the options vest, these options are forfeited as they occur.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="background-color: white">On December
7, 2021, the Board of Directors authorized the Company&#8217;s Equit</span>y Incentive Plan in order to facilitate the grant of equity
incentives to employees (including our named executive officers), directors, independent contractors, merchants, referral partners, channel
partners and employees of the Company&#8217;s to enable the Company to attract, retain and motivate employees, directors, merchants, referral
partners and channel partners, which is essential to its long-term success. A total of <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized_iI_pip0_c20251231__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--EquityIncentivePlanMember_zdkc5EmIR2Ma" title="Number of shares authorized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_EquityIncentivePlanMember" id="Fact001069" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">2,702,632</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock were previously
authorized under the Company&#8217;s Equity Incentive Plan. As of December 31, 2025, <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsForfeituresAndExpirationsInPeriod_pip0_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--TransactionTypeAxis__custom--EmployeeTerminationsMember_z6PtjS4K2rl5" title="Options cancelled, shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsForfeituresAndExpirationsInPeriod" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_custom_EmployeeTerminationsMember" id="Fact001071" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">1,029,842</ix:nonFraction></span> shares were cancelled as part of employee
terminations and <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--SharesIssued_iI_pip0_c20251231_zk7AFjsXz52a" title="Shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact001073" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">1,250,000</ix:nonFraction></span>  shares were added to the plan as part of the shareholder meeting. In total as of December 31, 2025, <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAvailableForGrant_iI_pip0_c20251231__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--EquityIncentivePlanMember_zh08vC45BW09" title="Number of shares available for issuance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAvailableForGrant" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_EquityIncentivePlanMember" id="Fact001075" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">2,788,009</ix:nonFraction></span>
shares are available for issuance.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="background-color: white">On March
20, 2024, the Company extended the expiration term of vested a</span>nd outstanding stock options to 10 years from the original grant
date for current employees and consultants, which resulted in an option modification. Also, certain terminated or separated option holders
were allowed to retain their vested options through the original expiration date following their termination. The fair value was calculated
both on the modification date and prior to the modification. The Company recorded the option modification expense of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockOptionPlanExpense_pn3n3_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--TransactionTypeAxis__custom--ModificationMember_zyzipdciHOVj" title="Stock option plan expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockOptionPlanExpense" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_custom_ModificationMember" id="Fact001077" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">325</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In May 2024, shareholders approved an additional
950,000 shares under a newly adopted 2024 Equity Incentive Plan (the "2024 Plan"), which replaced the previous plan. As a result,
a total of <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized_iI_pip0_c20241231__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--EquityIncentivePlan2024Member_zgi35g4hc8E9" title="Number of shares authorized"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAuthorized" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_EquityIncentivePlan2024Member" id="Fact001079" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">2,702,632</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock were authorized under the 2024 Plan, with <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAvailableForGrant_iI_pip0_c20241231__us-gaap--PlanNameAxis__custom--EquityIncentivePlan2024Member_zDobqYpxH6X8" title="Number of shares available for issuance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardNumberOfSharesAvailableForGrant" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_EquityIncentivePlan2024Member" id="Fact001081" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">508,167</ix:nonFraction></span> shares available for issuance as of December
31, 2024.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As part of the acquisition of FinZeo, the previous
management team received <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--NoncashOrPartNoncashAcquisitionNoncashFinancialOrEquityInstrumentConsiderationOptionsIssued1_pip0_c20240101__20241231__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--FinZeoMember_zl7lUuXIFVTk" title="Number of options issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NoncashOrPartNoncashAcquisitionNoncashFinancialOrEquityInstrumentConsiderationOptionsIssued1" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31_custom_FinZeoMember" id="Fact001083" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">1,500,000</ix:nonFraction></span> &#160;options of AppTech's common stock, which vest if the Company achieves certain sales targets.
One million in options were forfeited by the Seller upon his termination in June 2024 and canceled by the Company. No expense for the

of vesting.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The unvested options include a total of <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsNonvestedNumber_iI_pn3n3_c20251231__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__custom--SalesMilestonesMember_z56us9Gm27Vb" title="Options unvested"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardEquityInstrumentsOtherThanOptionsNonvestedNumber" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SalesMilestonesMember" id="Fact001170" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="Shares">1,425</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand options contingent upon reaching specified sales milestones.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">During the year ended December 31, 2025, the Company
recorded $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--StockOptionPlanExpense_pn3n3_c20250101__20251231_zsF20mOuFP7j" title="Option plan expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockOptionPlanExpense" contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" id="Fact001172" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">976</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand in option expenses, which includes the modification expense noted above of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockOptionPlanExpense_pn3n3_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--TransactionTypeAxis__custom--ModificationOfTermsMember_z7JNhkt8FTSi" title="Option plan expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockOptionPlanExpense" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_custom_ModificationOfTermsMember" id="Fact001174" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">49</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company recorded $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--EmployeeBenefitsAndShareBasedCompensation_pn3n3_c20240101__20241231_zS27XiYouzO5" title="Option expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EmployeeBenefitsAndShareBasedCompensation" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31" id="Fact001176" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,125</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand in option
expenses, which includes the modification expense of $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--EmployeeBenefitsAndShareBasedCompensation_pn3n3_c20240101__20241231__us-gaap--TransactionTypeAxis__custom--ModificationExpenseMember_z0ipHOvxHfp2" title="Option expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EmployeeBenefitsAndShareBasedCompensation" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31_custom_ModificationExpenseMember" id="Fact001178" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">325</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand and the company-wide grant of <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodGross_pip0_c20240101__20241231__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--StockOptionMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__custom--CompanyWideGrantMember_zjoySjKADEbh" title="Shares granted"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ShareBasedCompensationArrangementByShareBasedPaymentAwardOptionsGrantsInPeriodGross" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31_us-gaap_StockOptionMember_custom_CompanyWideGrantMember" id="Fact001180" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="0" unitRef="Shares">406,000</ix:nonFraction></span> options valued at $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--EmployeeBenefitsAndShareBasedCompensation_pn3n3_c20240101__20241231__us-gaap--AwardTypeAxis__us-gaap--StockOptionMember__us-gaap--TransactionTypeAxis__custom--CompanyWideGrantMember_znMuc4KRj3m" title="Option expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EmployeeBenefitsAndShareBasedCompensation" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31_us-gaap_StockOptionMember_custom_CompanyWideGrantMember187130203" id="Fact001182" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">349</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand
during the year ended December 31, 2024.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

options pricing model </span>with the following range of assumptions:</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8AB_zxpPblzd1lKh" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>Expected Volatility &#8211; </i>The Company
now has sufficient operating history and company-specific historical data to determine its expected volatility assumption directly from
its own traded stock price. Rather than examining the historical volatilities of a group of industry peers, the Company calculates its
volatility based on the fluctuations in its own share prices, which are publicly available. The Company expects to continue using this
approach going forward, relying on its own historical stock price data to assess volatility.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>&#160;</i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>Expected Term &#8211; </i>The expected term
of stock options represents the weighted average period the stock options are expected to be outstanding. Given the limited historical
exercise data of the Company&#8217;s stock options, the Company uses the simplified method for estimating the expected term, which calculates
the expected term as the average time-to-vesting and the contractual life of the options for stock options issued to participants.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>Risk-Free Interest Rate &#8211; </i>The risk-free
rate assumption is based on U.S. Treasury instruments, the terms of which were consistent with the expected term of the Company&#8217;s
stock options.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>&#160;</i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>Expected Dividend &#8211; </i>The expected
dividend assumption is based on the Company&#8217;s history and expectation of dividend payouts. The Company has not paid any dividends
to date and does not intend to pay dividends.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Forfeitures are recognized as they occur. Plan
related forfeitures are added back to the equity incentive plan.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Warrants</span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

warrants outstanding. The following table summarizes warrant activity:</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A4_znA8YyCBldN" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Income taxes paid (net of refunds received) were
$<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxPaidFederalAfterRefundReceived_c20250101__20251231_z6LdbUDWkOJk" title="Net of refunds received"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPaidFederalAfterRefundReceived" contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" id="Fact001306" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">0</ix:nonFraction></span> in federal, state, and foreign jurisdictions for both years. No individual jurisdiction exceeded 5% of total income taxes paid (net
of refunds).</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company&#8217;s net deferred tax assets at
December&#160;31, 2025 and 2024 was $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--DeferredTaxAssetsNet_iI_pn3n3_c20251231_zlTZQzVIETXa" title="Net deferred tax assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredTaxAssetsNet" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact001308" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">9,998</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand and $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--DeferredTaxAssetsNet_iI_pn3n3_c20241231_zVbPuVADOT38" title="Net deferred tax assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DeferredTaxAssetsNet" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31" id="Fact001310" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">8,562</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand, respectively, which primarily consists of net operating loss
carry forwards and accrued expenses. The Company has recorded a full valuation allowance against its deferred tax assets as of both periods
because management has determined that it is more likely than not that these assets will not be realized.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">For the years ended December 31, 2025 and
2024, the valuation allowance increased by approximately $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxReconciliationChangeInDeferredTaxAssetsValuationAllowance_pn3n3_c20250101__20251231_zWU0rFsu1Bk6" title="Valuation allowance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxReconciliationChangeInDeferredTaxAssetsValuationAllowance" contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" id="Fact001312" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,436</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand and $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxReconciliationChangeInDeferredTaxAssetsValuationAllowance_pn3n3_c20240101__20241231_zkAcP9gAug9d" title="Valuation allowance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxReconciliationChangeInDeferredTaxAssetsValuationAllowance" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31" id="Fact001314" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,462</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand, respectively. The total net operating loss carryforwards at December 31, 2025 was
$<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--OperatingLossCarryforwards_iI_pn3n3_c20251231_zbKO3umDiXN1" title="Valuation allowance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingLossCarryforwards" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact001316" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">47,572</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand. These net operating loss carryforwards begin to expire in 2027. Net operating losses incurred after 2020 can be carried
forward indefinitely.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company&#8217;s effective tax rate was <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations_dp_c20250101__20251231_zee9Zwcqh9Kl" title="Effective tax rate"><span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations_dp_c20240101__20241231_zA8xmDgnL6Nb" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations" contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" id="Fact001318" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations" contextRef="From2024-01-012024-12-31" id="Fact001320" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">0</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span>%
for both years.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company operates primarily in the United States
and files income tax returns in the U.S. federal jurisdiction and various state jurisdictions, including California. The Company is subject
to examination by U.S. federal and state taxing authorities for tax years beginning in 2020 and is not currently under examination by
any taxing authority.</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" escape="true" id="Fact001351" name="us-gaap:SubsequentEventsTextBlock"><p id="xdx_802_eus-gaap--SubsequentEventsTextBlock_zaVtj7OlLG7a" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b>NOTE 11 &#8211; <span id="xdx_820_zGCJIjXb4njd">SUBSEQUENT EVENTS</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Board ratified acceleration of previously
granted stock options for certain directors and executive officers (effective January 16, 2026). It approved issuance of 700,000 fully
vested stock options to certain directors (2025 performance and one-time board-service awards) and 1,500,000 fully vested restricted shares
to certain executives with a lock up period that extends until December 31, 2027. New grants of 850,000 stock options to certain executive
officers were approved with a vesting period that extends until December 31, 2027 along with related salary increases. The Board also
approved in principle the 2026 Incentive Compensation Plan, which ties payouts to EBITDA performance. The exercise price of the options
was $0.335 per share.</p>
